- **prove-it**: Prove work is done before claiming it — run every check fresh and show the output as evidence. Use before saying a story or fix is complete, before opening a PR, and whenever about to report success. (Small commits inside a TDD loop don't need the full run.) ([Pra-nav-G/dev-workflow-template](https://github.com/Pra-nav-G/dev-workflow-template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/prove-it) · 2 copies)
- **prove-it**: StarShow 项目的实证验收与测试门禁 Skill（Prove-It Verification Protocol）。 任何代码改动、Bug 修复、阶段里程碑（Phase 1~6）交付或宣称“已完成”前必须激活本 Skill。 杜绝“编译通过即完成”和 AI 偷懒弱化测试断言，强制要求实跑测试、审计 git diff、 验证并发零超卖与魔鬼故障剧本、执行脱敏扫描，并出具结构化《实证验收回执》。 ([qinchen1314/StarShow](https://github.com/qinchen1314/StarShow/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/prove-it))

- **prove-it-bites**: Writes a unit test and proves it actually catches the defect it claims to, by planting the defect, watching the test fail, restoring, and watching it pass. Chain with: build-lane for the code change the test covers; adversarial-review to check the plant/verify method itself under scrutiny. NOT for: writing production code beyond what's needed to plant/restore a defect; running a live-writing e2e suite; board writes.
